OFFICIAL GAZETTE — GOVT. OF GOA 1
SERIES I No. 28 ( EXTRAORDINARY ) 13TH OCTOBER, 2006
Panaji, 31st March, 2012 (Chaitra 11, 1934) SERIES II No. 52

EXTRAORDINARY

GOVERNMENT OF GOA

Department of Panchayati Raj and Community Development

Directorate of Panchayats
___

Notification

No. 19/1/DP/PAN/DIL/12/1126

This is to notify for information of the public that it is proposed to hold General Elections to the
Village Panchayats in Pernem Block/Taluka. The division of Village Panchayats into wards,
extent and limits of each ward and reservation of seats as per the provisions of Section 7 of the
Goa Panchayat Raj Act, 1994 (Goa Act No. 14 of 1994) is shown below:-
